'use server'

import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import { getPgPool } from '@/lib/db'
import { getServerUserScope } from '@/lib/context/server-user-scope'
import { recordAuditEvent } from '@/lib/audit'
import { uploadStudentPhotoToStorage } from '@/lib/storage/supabase-storage'
import type {
  Student,
  StudentEnrollment,
  StudentWithEnrollment,
  StudentFilterParams,
  UpsertStudentInput,
  EnrollStudentInput,
  CreateStudentWithEnrollmentInput,
} from '@/lib/types/students'

export type {
  Student,
  StudentEnrollment,
  StudentWithEnrollment,
  StudentFilterParams,
  UpsertStudentInput,
  EnrollStudentInput,
  CreateStudentWithEnrollmentInput,
}

/**
 * Checks whether the current user has access to view or manage a class cohort.
 */
function isClassAuthorized(assignedClasses: string[], isPrivileged: boolean, classIdOrName: string): boolean {
  if (isPrivileged) return true
  const norm = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-_]/g, '')
  const target = norm(classIdOrName)
  return assignedClasses.some((c) => norm(c) === target)
}

/**
 * Resolves a class identifier (id or name) to both canonical id and name from public.classes.
 */
async function resolveCanonicalClass(dbOrSupabase: any, classIdOrName: string): Promise<{ id: string; name: string } | null> {
  const norm = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().trim()
  let classRows: any[] = []
  if (typeof dbOrSupabase?.query === 'function') {
    const res = await dbOrSupabase.query(`SELECT id, name FROM public.classes;`)
    classRows = res.rows || []
  } else if (typeof dbOrSupabase?.from === 'function') {
    const { data } = await dbOrSupabase.from('classes').select('id, name')
    classRows = data || []
  }
  if (!classRows || classRows.length === 0) return null

  const target = norm(classIdOrName)
  const match = classRows.find(
    (c: any) => norm(c.id) === target || norm(c.name) === target || norm(c.id) === target.replace(/[\s-_]/g, '')
  )

  return match || null
}

/**
 * Standardizes revalidation across Admin, Roster, Dashboard, and Workstation views.
 */
function revalidateStudentPaths() {
  revalidatePath('/admin')
  revalidatePath('/admin', 'layout')
  revalidatePath('/maintenance')
  revalidatePath('/maintenance/workstations')
  revalidatePath('/records')
  revalidatePath('/', 'layout')
}

/**
 * Returns students enrolled in a specific class for an academic year, ordered by roll number ascending.
 * Enforces server-side class scope authorization.
 */
export async function getStudentsByClass(params: {
  classId: string
  academicYearId?: string
  statusFilter?: 'active' | 'archived' | 'all'
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; data: StudentWithEnrollment[]; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
      return { success: false, data: [], error: 'Access denied: Account not approved.' }
    }

    if (!isClassAuthorized(scope.assignedClasses, scope.isPrivileged, params.classId)) {
      return { success: false, data: [], error: `Access denied: You do not have permission to view roster for ${params.classId}.` }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()

    // Resolve canonical class ID
    const canonicalClass = await resolveCanonicalClass(pool, params.classId)
    const effectiveClassId = canonicalClass?.id || params.classId

    // Resolve target academic year (default to current active year if unspecified)
    let targetYearId = params.academicYearId
    if (!targetYearId) {
      const yearRes = await pool.query(`SELECT id FROM public.academic_years WHERE is_current = true LIMIT 1;`)
      targetYearId = yearRes.rows[0]?.id
      if (!targetYearId) {
        return { success: false, data: [], error: 'No active academic year configured. Please select or configure an academic year in Settings.' }
      }
    }

    const filter = params.statusFilter || 'active'
    let statusClause = `AND se.status IN ('active', 'completed', 'graduated')`
    if (filter === 'archived') {
      statusClause = `AND se.status IN ('archived', 'transferred')`
    } else if (filter === 'all') {
      statusClause = `AND se.status IN ('active', 'completed', 'graduated', 'archived', 'transferred')`
    }

    const query = `
      SELECT 
        se.id AS enrollment_id,
        se.academic_year_id,
        se.class_id,
        se.roll_number,
        se.section,
        se.status AS enrollment_status,
        s.id AS student_id,
        s.admission_no,
        s.registration_no,
        s.full_name,
        s.gender,
        s.is_active,
        s.photo_url,
        w.label AS assigned_workstation_label
      FROM public.student_enrollments se
      JOIN public.students s ON s.id = se.student_id
      LEFT JOIN public.workstation_seat_plans wsp ON wsp.enrollment_id = se.id
      LEFT JOIN public.workstations w ON w.id = wsp.workstation_id
      WHERE se.academic_year_id = $1
        AND se.class_id = $2
        ${statusClause}
      ORDER BY se.roll_number ASC;
    `
    const res = await pool.query(query, [targetYearId, effectiveClassId])

    const formatted: StudentWithEnrollment[] = res.rows.map((row: any) => {
      const wsLabel = row.assigned_workstation_label || null
      return {
        student_id: row.student_id,
        enrollment_id: row.enrollment_id,
        full_name: row.full_name,
        roll_number: row.roll_number,
        admission_no: row.admission_no,
        registration_no: row.registration_no,
        gender: row.gender,
        class_id: row.class_id,
        academic_year_id: row.academic_year_id,
        section: row.section,
        enrollment_status: row.enrollment_status,
        is_active: row.is_active,
        assigned_workstation_label: wsLabel,
        is_unseated: !wsLabel && row.enrollment_status === 'active',
        photo_url: row.photo_url || null,
      }
    })

    return { success: true, data: formatted }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, data: [], error: msg }
  }
}


/**
 * Creates or updates a student person record and enrolls them into a cohort.
 * Privileged users only.
 */
export async function createStudentWithEnrollment(
  input: CreateStudentWithEnrollmentInput
): Promise<{ success: boolean; data?: { studentId: string; enrollmentId: string }; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can create and enroll students.' }
  }

  const { student, enrollment } = input
  if (!student.full_name || !student.full_name.trim()) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Student full name is required.' }
  }
  if (!enrollment.academic_year_id || !enrollment.class_id || !enrollment.roll_number) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Academic year, class, and roll number are required.' }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    // 1. Resolve canonical class ID
    const canonicalClass = await resolveCanonicalClass(supabase, enrollment.class_id)
    const effectiveClassId = canonicalClass?.id || enrollment.class_id

    // Atomic execution via PostgreSQL stored function (guarantees rollback on error)
    const { data: rpcRes, error: rpcErr } = await (supabase.rpc as any)('create_student_with_enrollment_tx', {
      p_full_name: student.full_name.trim(),
      p_academic_year_id: enrollment.academic_year_id,
      p_class_id: effectiveClassId,
      p_roll_number: enrollment.roll_number,
      p_admission_no: student.admission_no?.trim() || null,
      p_gender: student.gender || null,
      p_section: enrollment.section || null,
      p_guardian_name: student.guardian_name?.trim() || null,
      p_guardian_phone: student.guardian_phone?.trim() || null,
      p_email: student.email?.trim() || null,
    })

    if (rpcErr) {
      if (rpcErr.message.includes('ROLL_COLLISION')) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: `Roll number ${enrollment.roll_number} is already assigned in ${canonicalClass?.name || effectiveClassId} for academic year ${enrollment.academic_year_id}.`,
        }
      }
      return { success: false, error: rpcErr.message }
    }

    revalidateStudentPaths()
    return {
      success: true,
      data: {
        studentId: rpcRes.student_id,
        enrollmentId: rpcRes.enrollment_id,
      },
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Creates or updates a student person entity. Privileged only.
 */
export async function upsertStudent(
  input: UpsertStudentInput
): Promise<{ success: boolean; data?: Student; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can modify student identities.' }
  }

  if (!input.full_name || !input.full_name.trim()) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Student full name is required.' }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    if (input.id) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('students')
        .update({
          full_name: input.full_name.trim(),
          admission_no: input.admission_no?.trim() || null,
          registration_no: input.registration_no?.trim() || null,
          gender: input.gender || null,
          dob_bs: input.dob_bs || null,
          dob_ad: input.dob_ad || null,
          guardian_name: input.guardian_name?.trim() || null,
          guardian_phone: input.guardian_phone?.trim() || null,
          email: input.email?.trim() || null,
        })
        .eq('id', input.id)
        .select('*')
        .single()

      if (error) return { success: false, error: error.message }
      return { success: true, data: data as Student }
    } else {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('students')
        .insert({
          full_name: input.full_name.trim(),
          admission_no: input.admission_no?.trim() || null,
          registration_no: input.registration_no?.trim() || null,
          gender: input.gender || null,
          dob_bs: input.dob_bs || null,
          dob_ad: input.dob_ad || null,
          guardian_name: input.guardian_name?.trim() || null,
          guardian_phone: input.guardian_phone?.trim() || null,
          email: input.email?.trim() || null,
          is_active: true,
        })
        .select('*')
        .single()

      if (error) return { success: false, error: error.message }
      return { success: true, data: data as Student }
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Updates an enrollment's status or roll number without deleting historical context.
 */
export async function updateEnrollment(
  enrollmentId: string,
  updates: {
    roll_number?: number
    section?: string | null
    status?: 'active' | 'transferred' | 'graduated' | 'archived'
  }
): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can update student enrollments.' }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    // If updating roll_number, verify no collision
    if (updates.roll_number) {
      const { data: currentEnrollment } = await supabase
        .from('student_enrollments')
        .select('academic_year_id, class_id, roll_number')
        .eq('id', enrollmentId)
        .single()

      if (currentEnrollment && currentEnrollment.roll_number !== updates.roll_number) {
        const { data: collision } = await supabase
          .from('student_enrollments')
          .select('id')
          .eq('academic_year_id', currentEnrollment.academic_year_id)
          .eq('class_id', currentEnrollment.class_id)
          .eq('roll_number', updates.roll_number)
          .neq('id', enrollmentId)
          .maybeSingle()

        if (collision) {
          return { success: false, error: `Roll number ${updates.roll_number} is already assigned in this cohort.` }
        }
      }
    }

    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('student_enrollments')
      .update(updates)
      .eq('id', enrollmentId)

    if (error) return { success: false, error: error.message }

    revalidateStudentPaths()
    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Soft deactivates a student record. Historical academic history and enrollments remain intact.
 */
export async function softDeleteStudent(
  studentId: string,
  reason?: string
): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can archive students.' }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('students')
      .update({
        is_active: false,
        archived_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        archive_reason: reason || 'Archived by administrator',
      })
      .eq('id', studentId)

    if (error) return { success: false, error: error.message }

    revalidateStudentPaths()
    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Transfers a student from one class to another without destroying historical enrollment records.
 */
export async function transferStudentEnrollment(input: {
  studentId: string
  fromClassId: string
  toClassId: string
  academicYearId: string
  newRollNumber: number
  section?: string | null
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; newEnrollmentId?: string; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can transfer students.' }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    // 1. Resolve canonical class IDs
    const fromClass = await resolveCanonicalClass(supabase, input.fromClassId)
    const toClass = await resolveCanonicalClass(supabase, input.toClassId)
    const effectiveFromClassId = fromClass?.id || input.fromClassId
    const effectiveToClassId = toClass?.id || input.toClassId

    // 2. Check collision in destination class
    const { data: collision } = await supabase
      .from('student_enrollments')
      .select('id')
      .eq('academic_year_id', input.academicYearId)
      .eq('class_id', effectiveToClassId)
      .eq('roll_number', input.newRollNumber)
      .eq('status', 'active')
      .maybeSingle()

    if (collision) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `Roll number ${input.newRollNumber} is already occupied in destination class ${toClass?.name || effectiveToClassId}.`,
      }
    }

    // 3. Mark previous enrollment as 'transferred' (preserves historical record)
    await supabase
      .from('student_enrollments')
      .update({ status: 'transferred', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('academic_year_id', input.academicYearId)
      .eq('class_id', effectiveFromClassId)
      .eq('student_id', input.studentId)

    // 4. Create new active enrollment in destination class
    const { data: newEnrollment, error: enrollErr } = await supabase
      .from('student_enrollments')
      .insert({
        academic_year_id: input.academicYearId,
        class_id: effectiveToClassId,
        student_id: input.studentId,
        roll_number: input.newRollNumber,
        section: input.section || null,
        status: 'active',
      })
      .select('id')
      .single()

    if (enrollErr) {
      return { success: false, error: enrollErr.message }
    }

    revalidateStudentPaths()
    return { success: true, newEnrollmentId: newEnrollment?.id }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown transfer error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Updates a student's personal identity and their cohort enrollment details.
 * Privileged users only.
 */
export async function updateStudentDetailsAction(input: {
  studentId: string
  enrollmentId: string
  fullName: string
  rollNumber: number
  admissionNo?: string | null
  registrationNo?: string | null
  gender?: string | null
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can edit student records.' }
  }

  const { studentId, enrollmentId, fullName, rollNumber, admissionNo, registrationNo, gender } = input

  if (!fullName || !fullName.trim()) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Student full name is required.' }
  }

  if (!rollNumber || rollNumber <= 0 || rollNumber > 200) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Roll number must be between 1 and 200.' }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const client = await pool.connect()

  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN')

    // 1. Fetch current enrollment
    const enrRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT academic_year_id, class_id, roll_number FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE id = $1;`,
      [enrollmentId]
    )

    if (enrRes.rows.length === 0) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      return { success: false, error: 'Enrollment record not found.' }
    }

    const currentEnrollment = enrRes.rows[0]

    // 2. Check roll collision in this cohort
    if (currentEnrollment.roll_number !== rollNumber) {
      const collRes = await client.query(
        `SELECT se.id, se.roll_number, s.full_name
         FROM public.student_enrollments se
         JOIN public.students s ON s.id = se.student_id
         WHERE se.academic_year_id = $1 
           AND se.class_id = $2 
           AND se.roll_number = $3 
           AND se.status = 'active'
           AND se.id != $4
         LIMIT 1;`,
        [currentEnrollment.academic_year_id, currentEnrollment.class_id, rollNumber, enrollmentId]
      )

      if (collRes.rows.length > 0) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK')
        const occupantName = collRes.rows[0].full_name || 'another student'
        return {
          success: false,
          error: `Roll ${rollNumber} is already occupied by ${occupantName}. Please choose an unassigned roll number.`,
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Pre-save check: admission_no uniqueness across students
    if (admissionNo && admissionNo.trim()) {
      const cleanAdm = admissionNo.trim()
      const admRes = await client.query(
        `SELECT id, full_name, admission_no
         FROM public.students
         WHERE LOWER(admission_no) = LOWER($1)
           AND id != $2
         LIMIT 1;`,
        [cleanAdm, studentId]
      )

      if (admRes.rows.length > 0) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK')
        return {
          success: false,
          error: `Admission number "${cleanAdm}" is already assigned to ${admRes.rows[0].full_name}.`,
        }
      }
    }

    // 4. Update student identity
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE public.students
       SET full_name = $1,
           admission_no = $2,
           registration_no = $3,
           gender = $4,
           is_active = true
       WHERE id = $5;`,
      [
        fullName.trim(),
        admissionNo?.trim() || null,
        registrationNo?.trim() || null,
        (gender as string) || null,
        studentId,
      ]
    )

    // 5. Update enrollment
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE public.student_enrollments
       SET roll_number = $1,
           updated_at = NOW()
       WHERE id = $2;`,
      [rollNumber, enrollmentId]
    )

    await client.query('COMMIT')

    revalidateStudentPaths()
    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK')
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown update error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  } finally {
    client.release()
  }
}

/**
 * Deletes a student enrollment and dummy person record if safe (zero attendance logs).
 * If attendance logs exist, archives enrollment to prevent corrupting academic logs.
 */
export async function deleteStudentEnrollmentAction(input: {
  enrollmentId: string
  studentId: string
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; actionTaken?: 'deleted' | 'archived'; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can delete or unenroll students.' }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const client = await pool.connect()

  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN')

    // 1. Check if enrollment has attendance records
    const attRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT count(*)::int AS cnt FROM public.log_attendance WHERE enrollment_id = $1;`,
      [input.enrollmentId]
    )
    const logCount = attRes.rows[0]?.cnt || 0

    if (logCount > 0) {
      // Archive instead of delete to protect historical records
      await client.query(
        `UPDATE public.student_enrollments
         SET status = 'archived', updated_at = NOW()
         WHERE id = $1;`,
        [input.enrollmentId]
      )

      // Immediately free up workstation seats
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.workstation_seat_plans WHERE enrollment_id = $1;`, [input.enrollmentId])
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.session_seat_overrides WHERE enrollment_id = $1;`, [input.enrollmentId])

      await client.query('COMMIT')
      revalidateStudentPaths()
      return {
        success: true,
        actionTaken: 'archived',
        error: `Student has ${logCount} recorded practical attendance logs. The enrollment was safely archived instead of deleted to protect historical compliance.`,
      }
    }

    // 2. Safe delete: clean up seat plans and enrollment
    await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.workstation_seat_plans WHERE enrollment_id = $1;`, [input.enrollmentId])
    await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.session_seat_overrides WHERE enrollment_id = $1;`, [input.enrollmentId])
    await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE id = $1;`, [input.enrollmentId])

    // 3. Delete student person if no other enrollments remain
    const remRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT count(*)::int AS cnt FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE student_id = $1;`,
      [input.studentId]
    )
    if (remRes.rows[0].cnt === 0) {
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.students WHERE id = $1;`, [input.studentId])
    }

    await client.query('COMMIT')
    revalidateStudentPaths()
    return { success: true, actionTaken: 'deleted' }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK')
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown delete error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  } finally {
    client.release()
  }
}

/**
 * Bulk archives multiple student enrollments in one transaction.
 */
export async function bulkArchiveStudentsAction(
  enrollmentIds: string[]
): Promise<{ success: boolean; count?: number; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can archive students.' }
  }

  if (!enrollmentIds || enrollmentIds.length === 0) {
    return { success: false, error: 'No students selected for archiving.' }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const client = await pool.connect()

  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN')

    await client.query(
      `UPDATE public.student_enrollments
       SET status = 'archived', updated_at = NOW()
       WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[]);`,
      [enrollmentIds]
    )

    // Immediately free up workstation seats for all archived enrollments
    await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.workstation_seat_plans WHERE enrollment_id = ANY($1::uuid[]);`, [enrollmentIds])
    await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.session_seat_overrides WHERE enrollment_id = ANY($1::uuid[]);`, [enrollmentIds])

    await client.query('COMMIT')

    revalidateStudentPaths()
    return { success: true, count: enrollmentIds.length }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK')
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  } finally {
    client.release()
  }
}

/**
 * Bulk deletes multiple student enrollments, cleanly removing zero-history students
 * and safely archiving those with attendance logs.
 */
export async function bulkDeleteStudentsAction(
  items: Array<{ enrollmentId: string; studentId: string }>
): Promise<{ success: boolean; deletedCount: number; archivedCount: number; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, deletedCount: 0, archivedCount: 0, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can delete students.' }
  }

  if (!items || items.length === 0) {
    return { success: false, deletedCount: 0, archivedCount: 0, error: 'No students selected.' }
  }

  try {
    let deletedCount = 0
    let archivedCount = 0

    for (const item of items) {
      const res = await deleteStudentEnrollmentAction(item)
      if (res.success) {
        if (res.actionTaken === 'deleted') deletedCount++
        else archivedCount++
      }
    }

    revalidateStudentPaths()
    return { success: true, deletedCount, archivedCount }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, deletedCount: 0, archivedCount: 0, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Restores an archived student enrollment back to active status.
 * Auto-resolves roll collisions if their old roll was taken while archived.
 */
export async function restoreStudentEnrollmentAction(
  enrollmentId: string,
  requestedRollNumber?: number
): Promise<{ success: boolean; newRollNumber?: number; restoredStudentName?: string; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can restore archived students.' }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const client = await pool.connect()

  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN')

    // 1. Fetch current enrollment details
    const enrRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT se.id, se.student_id, se.academic_year_id, se.class_id, se.roll_number, se.status, s.full_name
       FROM public.student_enrollments se
       JOIN public.students s ON s.id = se.student_id
       WHERE se.id = $1;`,
      [enrollmentId]
    )

    if (enrRes.rows.length === 0) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      return { success: false, error: 'Archived enrollment record not found.' }
    }

    const enrollment = enrRes.rows[0]
    const studentName = enrollment.full_name || 'Student'

    if (enrollment.status === 'active') {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      return { success: true, restoredStudentName: studentName }
    }

    let targetRoll = requestedRollNumber ?? enrollment.roll_number

    // 2. Validate roll collision with active students in same cohort
    const activeRollsRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT roll_number FROM public.student_enrollments
       WHERE academic_year_id = $1 AND class_id = $2 AND status = 'active' AND id != $3;`,
      [enrollment.academic_year_id, enrollment.class_id, enrollmentId]
    )

    const occupiedRolls = new Set(activeRollsRes.rows.map((r: any) => r.roll_number))

    let rollChanged = false
    if (occupiedRolls.has(targetRoll)) {
      const maxRoll = occupiedRolls.size > 0 ? Math.max(...Array.from(occupiedRolls)) : 0
      targetRoll = maxRoll + 1
      rollChanged = true
    }

    // 3. Reactivate enrollment
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE public.student_enrollments
       SET status = 'active', roll_number = $1, updated_at = NOW()
       WHERE id = $2;`,
      [targetRoll, enrollmentId]
    )

    // 4. Ensure student person record is marked active
    await client.query(
      `UPDATE public.students
       SET is_active = true, archived_at = NULL, archive_reason = NULL
       WHERE id = $1;`,
      [enrollment.student_id]
    )

    await client.query('COMMIT')

    revalidateStudentPaths()

    return {
      success: true,
      restoredStudentName: studentName,
      newRollNumber: rollChanged ? targetRoll : undefined,
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK')
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown restore error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  } finally {
    client.release()
  }
}

/**
 * Alphabetically sorts active cohort students by full name (A-Z) and renumbers roll numbers 1..N.
 * Strictly blocked if ANY practical attendance logs exist for this cohort.
 */
export async function resequenceClassRollsAlphabeticallyAction(params: {
  classId: string
  academicYearId: string
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; count?: number; error?: string }> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only approved administrators can resequence roll numbers.' }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const client = await pool.connect()

  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN')

    // 1. Resolve canonical class
    const canonicalClass = await resolveCanonicalClass(pool, params.classId)
    const effectiveClassId = canonicalClass?.id || params.classId

    // 2. Fetch active enrollments with student names
    const enrRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT se.id, se.roll_number, se.student_id, s.full_name
       FROM public.student_enrollments se
       JOIN public.students s ON s.id = se.student_id
       WHERE se.academic_year_id = $1 
         AND se.class_id = $2 
         AND se.status = 'active';`,
      [params.academicYearId, effectiveClassId]
    )

    if (enrRes.rows.length === 0) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      return { success: false, error: 'No active students found in this class cohort to resequence.' }
    }

    const enrollments = enrRes.rows
    const enrollmentIds = enrollments.map((e: any) => e.id)

    // 3. Safety Guard: Check if ANY practical attendance logs exist for this cohort
    const attRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT count(*)::int AS cnt FROM public.log_attendance WHERE enrollment_id = ANY($1::uuid[]);`,
      [enrollmentIds]
    )
    const logCount = attRes.rows[0]?.cnt || 0

    if (logCount > 0) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `Cannot resequence roll numbers: ${logCount} practical attendance record(s) already exist for this class. Resequencing would invalidate historical attendance logs.`,
      }
    }

    // 4. Sort students alphabetically by full_name (A-Z)
    const sorted = [...enrollments].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
      const nameA = (a.full_name || '').trim().toLowerCase()
      const nameB = (b.full_name || '').trim().toLowerCase()
      return nameA.localeCompare(nameB, 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' })
    })

    // 5. Update roll numbers directly:
    // uq_enrollment_roll is DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED, so temporary duplicates
    // during sequential updates within the transaction are safely permitted until COMMIT.
    for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length; i++) {
      await client.query(
        `UPDATE public.student_enrollments SET roll_number = $1, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $2;`,
        [i + 1, sorted[i].id]
      )
    }

    await client.query('COMMIT')

    // Record audit event
    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'UPDATE',
      entityType: 'roster',
      entityId: `${params.academicYearId}-${effectiveClassId}`,
      entityLabel: `Alphabetical Roll Resequencing â€” ${canonicalClass?.name || effectiveClassId}`,
      after: {
        resequenced_count: sorted.length,
        class_id: effectiveClassId,
        academic_year_id: params.academicYearId,
      },
      metadata: {
        action: 'ALPHABETICAL_ROLL_SORT',
        actor_id: scope.userId,
        actor_name: scope.fullName,
      },
    })

    revalidateStudentPaths()

    return { success: true, count: sorted.length }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK')
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown resequence error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  } finally {
    client.release()
  }
}

/**
 * Super Admin Danger Zone: Purges all test data for a class cohort (practical logs, seat plans,
 * enrollments, and unattached test student records).
 * Strictly guarded by super_admin role and typed confirmation phrase.
 */
export async function wipeClassCohortDataAction(params: {
  classId: string
  academicYearId: string
  confirmPhrase: string
  reason?: string
}): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  receipt?: {
    logsDeleted: number
    enrollmentsDeleted: number
    studentsDeleted: number
  }
  error?: string
}> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.role !== 'super_admin' || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return {
      success: false,
      error: 'Unauthorized: Wiping class cohort data is restricted exclusively to approved Super Administrators.',
    }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const client = await pool.connect()

  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN')

    // 1. Resolve canonical class
    const canonicalClass = await resolveCanonicalClass(pool, params.classId)
    const effectiveClassId = canonicalClass?.id || params.classId
    const className = canonicalClass?.name || effectiveClassId

    // 2. Validate confirmation phrase: must be WIPE <CLASS_NAME> or WIPE <CLASS_ID>
    const expectedPhrase1 = `WIPE ${className.toUpperCase()}`
    const expectedPhrase2 = `WIPE ${effectiveClassId.toUpperCase()}`
    const normalizedInput = (params.confirmPhrase || '').trim().toUpperCase()

    if (normalizedInput !== expectedPhrase1 && normalizedInput !== expectedPhrase2) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `Confirmation phrase mismatch. Please type exactly: "${expectedPhrase1}"`,
      }
    }

    // 3. Find all enrollments in this class & year
    const enrRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT id, student_id FROM public.student_enrollments
       WHERE academic_year_id = $1 AND class_id = $2;`,
      [params.academicYearId, effectiveClassId]
    )

    const enrollmentIds = enrRes.rows.map((r: any) => r.id)
    const candidateStudentIds = Array.from(new Set(enrRes.rows.map((r: any) => r.student_id)))

    let logsDeleted = 0
    let enrollmentsDeleted = enrollmentIds.length
    let studentsDeleted = 0

    if (enrollmentIds.length > 0) {
      // Step A: Count and delete log_attendance
      const attRes = await client.query(
        `DELETE FROM public.log_attendance WHERE enrollment_id = ANY($1::uuid[]);`,
        [enrollmentIds]
      )
      logsDeleted = attRes.rowCount || 0

      // Step A2: Delete ghost practical logs matching this class cohort and academic year
      await client.query(`
        DELETE FROM public.practical_logs 
        WHERE (batch_group = $1 OR class_subject_id IN (SELECT id FROM public.class_subjects WHERE class_id = $1))
          AND (academic_year_id = $2 OR academic_year_id IS NULL);
      `, [effectiveClassId, params.academicYearId])

      // Step B: Delete workstation seats and overrides
      await client.query(
        `DELETE FROM public.workstation_seat_plans WHERE enrollment_id = ANY($1::uuid[]);`,
        [enrollmentIds]
      )
      await client.query(
        `DELETE FROM public.session_seat_overrides WHERE enrollment_id = ANY($1::uuid[]);`,
        [enrollmentIds]
      )

      // Step C: Delete enrollments
      await client.query(
        `DELETE FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[]);`,
        [enrollmentIds]
      )

      // Step D: Delete orphaned student person records (those with no remaining enrollments)
      for (const studentId of candidateStudentIds) {
        const remRes = await client.query(
          `SELECT count(*)::int AS cnt FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE student_id = $1;`,
          [studentId]
        )
        if (remRes.rows[0].cnt === 0) {
          await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.students WHERE id = $1;`, [studentId])
          studentsDeleted++
        }
      }
    }

    await client.query('COMMIT')

    // 4. Record destruction receipt in public.audit_logs
    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'DELETE',
      entityType: 'roster',
      entityId: `${params.academicYearId}-${effectiveClassId}`,
      entityLabel: `Class Cohort Purge (Danger Zone) â€” ${className}`,
      before: {
        academic_year_id: params.academicYearId,
        class_id: effectiveClassId,
        enrollments_purged: enrollmentsDeleted,
        attendance_logs_purged: logsDeleted,
        orphaned_students_purged: studentsDeleted,
      },
      metadata: {
        action: 'WIPE_CLASS_COHORT',
        reason: params.reason || 'Super Admin Danger Zone Wipe',
        actor_id: scope.userId,
        actor_name: scope.fullName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    })

    revalidatePath('/maintenance')
    revalidatePath('/maintenance/workstations')
    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/schedules')

    return {
      success: true,
      receipt: {
        logsDeleted,
        enrollmentsDeleted,
        studentsDeleted,
      },
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK')
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown wipe error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  } finally {
    client.release()
  }
}

/**
 * Updates the photo avatar for an individual student.
 */
export async function updateStudentPhotoAction(params: {
  studentId: string
  photoUrl: string | null
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
      return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Insufficient privileges to update student photo.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()
    const studentRes = await pool.query(
      `SELECT id, full_name, photo_url FROM public.students WHERE id = $1;`,
      [params.studentId]
    )

    if (studentRes.rows.length === 0) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Student record not found.' }
    }

    const prevStudent = studentRes.rows[0]

    let finalPhotoUrl = params.photoUrl
    if (finalPhotoUrl && (finalPhotoUrl.startsWith('data:image/') || !finalPhotoUrl.startsWith('http'))) {
      finalPhotoUrl = await uploadStudentPhotoToStorage(params.studentId, finalPhotoUrl)
    }

    await pool.query(
      `UPDATE public.students SET photo_url = $1, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $2;`,
      [finalPhotoUrl, params.studentId]
    )

    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'UPDATE',
      entityType: 'student',
      entityId: params.studentId,
      entityLabel: `Student Photo Update â€” ${prevStudent.full_name}`,
      before: { photo_url: prevStudent.photo_url },
      after: { photo_url: finalPhotoUrl ? 'UPDATED_IMAGE' : null },
      metadata: {
        actor_id: scope.userId,
        actor_name: scope.fullName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    })

    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/maintenance')
    revalidatePath('/records')

    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown photo update error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Atomically updates photo avatars for a batch of students (e.g. from Batch Photo Import).
 */
export async function batchUpdateStudentPhotosAction(params: {
  updates: Array<{ studentId: string; photoUrl: string }>
}): Promise<{ success: boolean; count: number; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
      return { success: false, count: 0, error: 'Access denied: Insufficient privileges for batch photo import.' }
    }

    if (!params.updates || params.updates.length === 0) {
      return { success: true, count: 0 }
    }

    // Process all photo uploads to Supabase Storage before DB transaction
    const processedUpdates = await Promise.all(
      params.updates.map(async (item) => {
        let finalUrl = item.photoUrl
        if (finalUrl && (finalUrl.startsWith('data:image/') || !finalUrl.startsWith('http'))) {
          finalUrl = await uploadStudentPhotoToStorage(item.studentId, finalUrl)
        }
        return { studentId: item.studentId, photoUrl: finalUrl }
      })
    )

    const pool = getPgPool()
    const client = await pool.connect()

    try {
      await client.query('BEGIN')

      for (const item of processedUpdates) {
        await client.query(
          `UPDATE public.students SET photo_url = $1, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $2;`,
          [item.photoUrl, item.studentId]
        )
      }

      await client.query('COMMIT')

      await recordAuditEvent({
        action: 'UPDATE',
        entityType: 'student',
        entityId: 'batch-photo-import',
        entityLabel: `Batch Student Photo Import (${processedUpdates.length} students)`,
        metadata: {
          total_updated: processedUpdates.length,
          actor_id: scope.userId,
          actor_name: scope.fullName,
          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
      })

      revalidatePath('/admin')
      revalidatePath('/maintenance')
      revalidatePath('/records')

      return { success: true, count: processedUpdates.length }
    } catch (batchErr) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      throw batchErr
    } finally {
      client.release()
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown batch photo update error'
    return { success: false, count: 0, error: msg }
  }
}

'use server'

import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'
import { getPgPool } from '@/lib/db'
import { getServerUserScope } from '@/lib/context/server-user-scope'
import { recordAuditEvent } from '@/lib/audit'
import type {
  ArchiveCategoryType,
  ArchivedStudentRecord,
  ArchivedAcademicYearRecord,
  ArchivedInfrastructureRecord,
  ArchiveHubTelemetry,
} from '@/lib/types/archive-hub'

/**
 * Standardizes path revalidations for all student, roster, and admin views.
 */
function revalidateArchivePaths() {
  revalidatePath('/admin')
  revalidatePath('/admin', 'layout')
  revalidatePath('/maintenance')
  revalidatePath('/records')
  revalidatePath('/', 'layout')
}

/**
 * Fetches high-level executive telemetry for the Institutional Recycle Bin & Archive Hub.
 */
export async function getArchiveHubTelemetryAction(): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  data?: ArchiveHubTelemetry
  error?: string
}> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only administrators can view archive telemetry.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()
    const res = await pool.query(`
      SELECT
        (
          SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT s.id)::int
          FROM public.students s
          LEFT JOIN public.student_enrollments se ON se.student_id = s.id
          WHERE (s.is_active = false OR se.status = 'archived')
            AND (se.status IS NULL OR se.status NOT IN ('completed', 'graduated', 'active'))
        ) AS trash_count,
        (
          SELECT COUNT(*)::int
          FROM public.student_enrollments
          WHERE status = 'graduated'
        ) AS graduated_count,
        (
          SELECT COUNT(*)::int
          FROM public.student_enrollments
          WHERE status = 'transferred'
        ) AS transferred_count,
        (
          SELECT COUNT(*)::int
          FROM public.academic_years
          WHERE is_current = false
        ) AS archived_years_count,
        (
          SELECT COUNT(*)::int
          FROM public.labs
          WHERE status = 'Inactive'
        ) AS inactive_labs_count,
        (
          SELECT COALESCE(
            (
              SELECT count(*)::int
              FROM (
                SELECT jsonb_array_elements(value) AS cat
                FROM public.institution_settings
                WHERE key = 'infrastructure_incident_categories'
              ) sub
              WHERE (cat->>'is_active')::boolean = false
            ),
            0
          )
        ) AS archived_categories_count;
    `)

    const row = res.rows[0] || {}
    const telemetry: ArchiveHubTelemetry = {
      trashCount: Number(row.trash_count || 0),
      graduatedCount: Number(row.graduated_count || 0),
      transferredCount: Number(row.transferred_count || 0),
      archivedYearsCount: Number(row.archived_years_count || 0),
      inactiveLabsCount: Number(row.inactive_labs_count || 0),
      archivedCategoriesCount: Number(row.archived_categories_count || 0),
    }

    return { success: true, data: telemetry }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error fetching archive telemetry'
    console.error('getArchiveHubTelemetryAction failed:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Queries archived student records with disambiguation between Recycle Bin (trash),
 * Graduated Alumni, and Transferred students.
 */
export async function getArchivedStudentsAction(params: {
  category: 'trash' | 'graduated' | 'transferred'
  search?: string
  academicYearId?: string
  classId?: string
  limit?: number
}): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  data?: ArchivedStudentRecord[]
  error?: string
}> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only administrators can access archived records.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()
    const { category, search, academicYearId, classId, limit = 200 } = params

    let whereClause = ''
    const queryParams: any[] = []

    if (category === 'trash') {
      whereClause = `(s.is_active = false OR se.status = 'archived') AND (se.status IS NULL OR se.status NOT IN ('completed', 'graduated', 'active'))`
    } else if (category === 'graduated') {
      whereClause = `se.status = 'graduated'`
    } else if (category === 'transferred') {
      whereClause = `se.status = 'transferred'`
    }

    if (search && search.trim()) {
      queryParams.push(`%${search.trim().toLowerCase()}%`)
      const pIdx = queryParams.length
      whereClause += ` AND (LOWER(s.full_name) LIKE $${pIdx} OR LOWER(COALESCE(s.admission_no, '')) LIKE $${pIdx})`
    }

    if (academicYearId && academicYearId !== 'all') {
      queryParams.push(academicYearId)
      const pIdx = queryParams.length
      whereClause += ` AND se.academic_year_id = $${pIdx}`
    }

    if (classId && classId !== 'all') {
      queryParams.push(classId)
      const pIdx = queryParams.length
      whereClause += ` AND se.class_id = $${pIdx}`
    }

    queryParams.push(limit)
    const limitIdx = queryParams.length

    const sql = `
      SELECT 
        s.id AS student_id,
        se.id AS enrollment_id,
        s.full_name,
        s.admission_no,
        s.registration_no,
        s.gender,
        s.is_active AS is_student_active,
        s.archived_at,
        s.archive_reason,
        se.class_id,
        c.name AS class_name,
        se.academic_year_id,
        ay.name AS academic_year_name,
        se.roll_number,
        se.section,
        COALESCE(se.status, 'archived') AS enrollment_status,
        COALESCE(
          (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM public.log_attendance la WHERE (se.id IS NOT NULL AND la.enrollment_id = se.id) OR la.student_id = s.id),
          0
        ) AS attendance_count
      FROM public.students s
      LEFT JOIN public.student_enrollments se ON se.student_id = s.id
      LEFT JOIN public.classes c ON c.id = se.class_id
      LEFT JOIN public.academic_years ay ON ay.id = se.academic_year_id
      WHERE ${whereClause}
      ORDER BY s.archived_at DESC NULLS LAST, s.full_name ASC
      LIMIT $${limitIdx};
    `

    const res = await pool.query(sql, queryParams)

    const data: ArchivedStudentRecord[] = res.rows.map((r: any) => ({
      studentId: String(r.student_id),
      enrollmentId: r.enrollment_id ? String(r.enrollment_id) : null,
      fullName: String(r.full_name),
      admissionNo: r.admission_no ? String(r.admission_no) : null,
      registrationNo: r.registration_no ? String(r.registration_no) : null,
      gender: r.gender ? String(r.gender) : null,
      classId: r.class_id ? String(r.class_id) : null,
      className: r.class_name ? String(r.class_name) : (r.class_id ? String(r.class_id) : null),
      academicYearId: r.academic_year_id ? String(r.academic_year_id) : null,
      academicYearName: r.academic_year_name ? String(r.academic_year_name) : (r.academic_year_id ? String(r.academic_year_id) : null),
      rollNumber: r.roll_number !== null ? Number(r.roll_number) : null,
      section: r.section ? String(r.section) : null,
      enrollmentStatus: String(r.enrollment_status),
      isStudentActive: Boolean(r.is_student_active),
      archivedAt: r.archived_at ? new Date(r.archived_at).toISOString() : null,
      archiveReason: r.archive_reason ? String(r.archive_reason) : null,
      attendanceCount: Number(r.attendance_count || 0),
      canPermanentPurge: Number(r.attendance_count || 0) === 0,
    }))

    return { success: true, data }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error querying archived students'
    console.error('getArchivedStudentsAction failed:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Queries past/archived academic years with operational statistics.
 */
export async function getArchivedAcademicYearsAction(): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  data?: ArchivedAcademicYearRecord[]
  error?: string
}> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only administrators can view past cycles.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()
    const res = await pool.query(`
      SELECT 
        ay.id,
        ay.name,
        ay.start_date,
        ay.end_date,
        ay.is_current,
        ay.is_archived,
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM public.practical_logs pl WHERE pl.academic_year_id = ay.id) AS total_logs,
        (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM public.student_enrollments se WHERE se.academic_year_id = ay.id) AS total_enrollments
      FROM public.academic_years ay
      WHERE ay.is_current = false
      ORDER BY ay.start_date DESC;
    `)

    const data: ArchivedAcademicYearRecord[] = res.rows.map((r: any) => ({
      id: String(r.id),
      name: String(r.name),
      startDate: r.start_date ? new Date(r.start_date).toISOString().split('T')[0] : '',
      endDate: r.end_date ? new Date(r.end_date).toISOString().split('T')[0] : '',
      isCurrent: Boolean(r.is_current),
      isArchived: Boolean(r.is_archived),
      totalLogs: Number(r.total_logs || 0),
      totalEnrollments: Number(r.total_enrollments || 0),
    }))

    return { success: true, data }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Queries inactive laboratories and soft-archived incident categories.
 */
export async function getArchivedInfrastructureAction(): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  data?: ArchivedInfrastructureRecord[]
  error?: string
}> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Access denied.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()
    const labRes = await pool.query(`
      SELECT id, 'lab' AS type, name, status, code AS code_or_severity, created_at
      FROM public.labs WHERE status = 'Inactive'
      ORDER BY created_at DESC;
    `)

    const data: ArchivedInfrastructureRecord[] = labRes.rows.map((r: any) => ({
      id: String(r.id),
      type: 'lab' as const,
      name: String(r.name),
      codeOrSeverity: r.code_or_severity ? String(r.code_or_severity) : undefined,
      status: String(r.status),
      createdAt: r.created_at ? new Date(r.created_at).toISOString() : new Date().toISOString(),
    }))

    const catRes = await pool.query(`
      SELECT value FROM public.institution_settings WHERE key = 'infrastructure_incident_categories';
    `)
    if (catRes.rows.length > 0 && Array.isArray(catRes.rows[0].value)) {
      for (const c of catRes.rows[0].value) {
        if (c && c.is_active === false) {
          data.push({
            id: String(c.id || c.code),
            type: 'incident_category',
            name: String(c.name),
            codeOrSeverity: c.severity ? String(c.severity) : (c.code ? String(c.code) : undefined),
            status: 'Archived',
            createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
        }
      }
    }

    return { success: true, data }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Restores a student and/or enrollment from the Recycle Bin back to active status.
 * Auto-resolves roll collision if their prior roll was assigned to another active student.
 */
export async function restoreStudentAction(input: {
  studentId: string
  enrollmentId?: string
  targetRollNumber?: number
}): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  restoredStudentName?: string
  newRollNumber?: number
  error?: string
}> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only administrators can restore archived items.' }
  }

  const { studentId, enrollmentId, targetRollNumber } = input
  if (!studentId) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Student ID is required.' }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const client = await pool.connect()

  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN')

    // 1. Reactivate student person record
    const sRes = await client.query(
      `UPDATE public.students
       SET is_active = true, archived_at = NULL, archive_reason = NULL
       WHERE id = $1
       RETURNING full_name;`,
      [studentId]
    )

    if (sRes.rows.length === 0) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      return { success: false, error: 'Student record not found.' }
    }

    const studentName = sRes.rows[0].full_name || 'Student'
    let assignedRoll: number | undefined = undefined

    // 2. If enrollmentId provided, reactivate enrollment
    if (enrollmentId) {
      const enrRes = await client.query(
        `SELECT id, student_id, academic_year_id, class_id, roll_number, status
         FROM public.student_enrollments
         WHERE id = $1;`,
        [enrollmentId]
      )

      if (enrRes.rows.length > 0) {
        const enr = enrRes.rows[0]
        let desiredRoll = targetRollNumber ?? enr.roll_number

        // Check active roll collisions
        const activeRollsRes = await client.query(
          `SELECT roll_number FROM public.student_enrollments
           WHERE academic_year_id = $1 AND class_id = $2 AND status = 'active' AND id != $3;`,
          [enr.academic_year_id, enr.class_id, enrollmentId]
        )

        const activeRolls = new Set(activeRollsRes.rows.map((r: any) => r.roll_number))
        if (activeRolls.has(desiredRoll)) {
          const maxRoll = activeRolls.size > 0 ? Math.max(...Array.from(activeRolls)) : 0
          desiredRoll = maxRoll + 1
        }
        assignedRoll = desiredRoll

        await client.query(
          `UPDATE public.student_enrollments
           SET status = 'active', roll_number = $1, updated_at = NOW()
           WHERE id = $2;`,
          [desiredRoll, enrollmentId]
        )
      }
    }

    await client.query('COMMIT')

    // Audit event
    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'UPDATE',
      entityType: 'student',
      entityId: studentId,
      entityLabel: `Restored Student: ${studentName}`,
      after: {
        is_active: true,
        restored_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        assigned_roll: assignedRoll,
      },
      metadata: {
        actionDesc: 'Restored student from Recycle Bin to active status',
      },
    })

    revalidateArchivePaths()

    return {
      success: true,
      restoredStudentName: studentName,
      newRollNumber: assignedRoll,
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK')
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown restore error'
    console.error('restoreStudentAction failed:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  } finally {
    client.release()
  }
}

/**
 * Ultra-safe permanent purge of a trash student item.
 * STRICT SAFETY RULE:
 * - If student has ANY recorded practical attendance logs, deletion is HARD BLOCKED to protect NEB compliance.
 * - Only items with 0 historical attendance logs can be permanently purged.
 */
export async function permanentPurgeTrashAction(input: {
  studentId: string
  enrollmentId?: string
}): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  error?: string
}> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only administrators can permanently purge records.' }
  }

  const { studentId, enrollmentId } = input
  if (!studentId) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Student ID is required.' }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  const client = await pool.connect()

  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN')

    // 1. Check student attendance logs across all enrollments
    const attRes = await client.query(
      `SELECT count(*)::int AS cnt
       FROM public.log_attendance
       WHERE enrollment_id IN (
         SELECT id FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE student_id = $1
       ) OR student_id = $1;`,
      [studentId]
    )

    const logCount = Number(attRes.rows[0]?.cnt || 0)
    if (logCount > 0) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `Permanent purge blocked: Student has ${logCount} recorded practical attendance logs. Under institutional and NEB regulations, operational logbook history cannot be deleted. This record will remain preserved in the archive.`,
      }
    }

    // 2. Fetch student name for audit
    const sRes = await client.query(`SELECT full_name FROM public.students WHERE id = $1;`, [studentId])
    const studentName = sRes.rows[0]?.full_name || studentId

    // 3. Clean up seat plans and session overrides
    if (enrollmentId) {
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.workstation_seat_plans WHERE enrollment_id = $1;`, [enrollmentId])
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.session_seat_overrides WHERE enrollment_id = $1;`, [enrollmentId])
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE id = $1;`, [enrollmentId])
    } else {
      await client.query(
        `DELETE FROM public.workstation_seat_plans WHERE enrollment_id IN (SELECT id FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE student_id = $1);`,
        [studentId]
      )
      await client.query(
        `DELETE FROM public.session_seat_overrides WHERE enrollment_id IN (SELECT id FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE student_id = $1);`,
        [studentId]
      )
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE student_id = $1;`, [studentId])
    }

    // 4. If no remaining enrollments for this person, delete student
    const remEnrRes = await client.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS cnt FROM public.student_enrollments WHERE student_id = $1;`, [studentId])
    if (Number(remEnrRes.rows[0]?.cnt || 0) === 0) {
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.students WHERE id = $1;`, [studentId])
    }

    await client.query('COMMIT')

    // Audit event
    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'DELETE',
      entityType: 'student',
      entityId: studentId,
      entityLabel: `Permanently Purged Student: ${studentName}`,
      before: { studentId, studentName },
      after: {},
      metadata: {
        actionDesc: 'Permanently purged zero-history student from Recycle Bin',
      },
    })

    revalidateArchivePaths()
    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK')
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown purge error'
    console.error('permanentPurgeTrashAction failed:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  } finally {
    client.release()
  }
}

/**
 * Restores an inactive lab or soft-archived incident category back to active service.
 */
export async function restoreInfrastructureItemAction(input: {
  itemType: 'lab' | 'incident_category'
  itemId: string
}): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  error?: string
}> {
  const scope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (!scope.isPrivileged) {
    return { success: false, error: 'Access denied: Only administrators can reactivate infrastructure.' }
  }

  const pool = getPgPool()
  try {
    if (input.itemType === 'lab') {
      const res = await pool.query(
        `UPDATE public.labs SET status = 'Operational', is_active = true WHERE id = $1 RETURNING name;`,
        [input.itemId]
      )
      if (res.rows.length === 0) return { success: false, error: 'Lab facility not found.' }

      await recordAuditEvent({
        action: 'UPDATE',
        entityType: 'lab',
        entityId: input.itemId,
        entityLabel: `Reactivated Lab: ${res.rows[0].name}`,
        after: { status: 'Operational', is_active: true },
        metadata: { actionDesc: 'Reactivated facility from archive' },
      })
    } else if (input.itemType === 'incident_category') {
      const catRes = await pool.query(
        `SELECT value FROM public.institution_settings WHERE key = 'infrastructure_incident_categories';`
      )
      if (catRes.rows.length === 0 || !Array.isArray(catRes.rows[0].value)) {
        return { success: false, error: 'Incident categories setting not found.' }
      }
      const list = catRes.rows[0].value
      const target = list.find((c: any) => String(c.id) === input.itemId || String(c.code) === input.itemId)
      if (!target) {
        return { success: false, error: 'Incident category not found.' }
      }
      target.is_active = true

      await pool.query(
        `UPDATE public.institution_settings SET value = $1::jsonb, updated_at = NOW() WHERE key = 'infrastructure_incident_categories';`,
        [JSON.stringify(list)]
      )

      await recordAuditEvent({
        action: 'UPDATE',
        entityType: 'incident_category',
        entityId: input.itemId,
        entityLabel: `Restored Incident Category: ${target.name}`,
        after: { is_active: true },
        metadata: { actionDesc: 'Restored incident category from archive' },
      })
    }

    revalidateArchivePaths()
    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown restore error'
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

=========================================
CODE FILE: app\actions\subjects.ts
=========================================

'use server'

import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'
import { getPgPool } from '@/lib/db'
import { getServerUserScope } from '@/lib/context/server-user-scope'
import type {
  SubjectRecord,
  CreateSubjectInput,
  UpdateSubjectInput,
  ClassSubjectRecord,
  AssignSubjectToClassInput,
} from '@/lib/types/subjects'

/**
 * Returns subjects from public.subjects database table.
 * Strictly dynamic single source of truth.
 */
export async function getSubjectsAction(filter?: {
  activeOnly?: boolean
  labId?: string
}): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  data: SubjectRecord[]
  error?: string
}> {
  try {
    const pool = getPgPool()
    const activeOnly = filter?.activeOnly !== false // defaults to true
    let query = `
      SELECT id, code, name, default_lab_id, credit_hours, is_active, created_at
      FROM public.subjects
      WHERE 1=1
    `
    const params: any[] = []

    if (activeOnly) {
      params.push(true)
      query += ` AND is_active = $${params.length}`
    }

    if (filter?.labId) {
      params.push(filter.labId)
      query += ` AND default_lab_id = $${params.length}`
    }

    query += ` ORDER BY name ASC;`

    const res = await pool.query(query, params)
    return {
      success: true,
      data: res.rows.map((r) => ({
        id: r.id,
        code: r.code,
        name: r.name,
        default_lab_id: r.default_lab_id,
        credit_hours: r.credit_hours !== null ? Number(r.credit_hours) : null,
        is_active: Boolean(r.is_active),
        created_at: r.created_at ? new Date(r.created_at).toISOString() : undefined,
      })),
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    console.error('Failed to get subjects from database:', msg)
    return { success: false, data: [], error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Creates a new subject in public.subjects with RBAC guard.
 */
export async function createSubjectAction(
  data: CreateSubjectInput
): Promise<{ success: boolean; data?: SubjectRecord; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
      return { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized: Only administrative faculty can create curriculum subjects.' }
    }

    const cleanCode = data.code.trim().toUpperCase()
    const cleanName = data.name.trim()
    if (!cleanCode || !cleanName) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Subject code and name are required.' }
    }

    const id = data.id?.trim().toLowerCase() || cleanCode.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, '-')
    const pool = getPgPool()

    // Check if code or id already exists
    const checkRes = await pool.query(
      `SELECT id FROM public.subjects WHERE id = $1 OR UPPER(code) = $2 LIMIT 1;`,
      [id, cleanCode]
    )
    if (checkRes.rows.length > 0) {
      return { success: false, error: `A subject with code "${cleanCode}" already exists.` }
    }

    const res = await pool.query(
      `
      INSERT INTO public.subjects (id, code, name, default_lab_id, credit_hours, is_active, created_at)
      VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, now())
      RETURNING id, code, name, default_lab_id, credit_hours, is_active, created_at;
      `,
      [
        id,
        cleanCode,
        cleanName,
        data.default_lab_id || null,
        data.credit_hours || null,
        data.is_active !== undefined ? data.is_active : true,
      ]
    )

    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/records')
    revalidatePath('/schedules')

    const r = res.rows[0]
    return {
      success: true,
      data: {
        id: r.id,
        code: r.code,
        name: r.name,
        default_lab_id: r.default_lab_id,
        credit_hours: r.credit_hours !== null ? Number(r.credit_hours) : null,
        is_active: Boolean(r.is_active),
        created_at: new Date(r.created_at).toISOString(),
      },
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    console.error('Failed to create subject:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Updates an existing subject in public.subjects with RBAC guard.
 */
export async function updateSubjectAction(
  id: string,
  data: UpdateSubjectInput
): Promise<{ success: boolean; data?: SubjectRecord; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
      return { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized: Only administrative faculty can update curriculum subjects.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()
    const updates: string[] = []
    const params: any[] = [id]

    if (data.code !== undefined) {
      params.push(data.code.trim().toUpperCase())
      updates.push(`code = $${params.length}`)
    }
    if (data.name !== undefined) {
      params.push(data.name.trim())
      updates.push(`name = $${params.length}`)
    }
    if (data.default_lab_id !== undefined) {
      params.push(data.default_lab_id || null)
      updates.push(`default_lab_id = $${params.length}`)
    }
    if (data.credit_hours !== undefined) {
      params.push(data.credit_hours !== null ? Number(data.credit_hours) : null)
      updates.push(`credit_hours = $${params.length}`)
    }
    if (data.is_active !== undefined) {
      params.push(Boolean(data.is_active))
      updates.push(`is_active = $${params.length}`)
    }

    if (updates.length === 0) {
      return { success: true }
    }

    const query = `
      UPDATE public.subjects
      SET ${updates.join(', ')}
      WHERE id = $1
      RETURNING id, code, name, default_lab_id, credit_hours, is_active, created_at;
    `
    const res = await pool.query(query, params)
    if (res.rows.length === 0) {
      return { success: false, error: `Subject not found.` }
    }

    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/records')
    revalidatePath('/schedules')

    const r = res.rows[0]
    return {
      success: true,
      data: {
        id: r.id,
        code: r.code,
        name: r.name,
        default_lab_id: r.default_lab_id,
        credit_hours: r.credit_hours !== null ? Number(r.credit_hours) : null,
        is_active: Boolean(r.is_active),
        created_at: new Date(r.created_at).toISOString(),
      },
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    console.error('Failed to update subject:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Soft-deactivates a subject with reference protection.
 * Foreign keys in practical_logs and schedules prevent raw DELETE.
 */
export async function deactivateSubjectAction(
  id: string
): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
      return { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized: Only administrative faculty can deactivate curriculum subjects.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()

    // Soft-deactivate to preserve historical integrity
    const res = await pool.query(
      `
      UPDATE public.subjects
      SET is_active = false
      WHERE id = $1
      RETURNING id;
      `,
      [id]
    )

    if (res.rows.length === 0) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Subject not found.' }
    }

    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/records')
    revalidatePath('/schedules')

    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    console.error('Failed to deactivate subject:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Returns relational class_subjects mappings enriched with class names,
 * subject names, and assigned teacher profiles.
 */
export async function getClassSubjectsAction(
  classId?: string,
  academicYearId?: string
): Promise<{
  success: boolean
  data: ClassSubjectRecord[]
  error?: string
}> {
  try {
    const pool = getPgPool()
    let query = `
      SELECT 
        cs.id,
        cs.academic_year_id,
        cs.class_id,
        cs.subject_id,
        cs.teacher_id,
        cs.default_lab_id,
        cs.created_at,
        c.name AS class_name,
        s.code AS subject_code,
        s.name AS subject_name,
        p.full_name AS teacher_name,
        l.name AS lab_name
      FROM public.class_subjects cs
      LEFT JOIN public.classes c ON c.id = cs.class_id
      LEFT JOIN public.subjects s ON s.id = cs.subject_id
      LEFT JOIN public.profiles p ON p.id = cs.teacher_id
      LEFT JOIN public.labs l ON l.id = cs.default_lab_id
      WHERE 1=1
    `
    const params: any[] = []

    if (classId) {
      params.push(classId)
      query += ` AND cs.class_id = $${params.length}`
    }

    let effectiveYearId = academicYearId
    if (!effectiveYearId) {
      const currentYearRes = await pool.query(
        `SELECT id FROM public.academic_years WHERE is_current = true LIMIT 1;`
      )
      effectiveYearId = currentYearRes.rows[0]?.id
    }

    if (effectiveYearId && effectiveYearId !== 'all') {
      params.push(effectiveYearId)
      query += ` AND (cs.academic_year_id = $${params.length} OR cs.academic_year_id IS NULL)`
    }

    query += ` ORDER BY c.name ASC, s.name ASC;`

    const res = await pool.query(query, params)
    return {
      success: true,
      data: res.rows.map((r) => ({
        id: r.id,
        academic_year_id: r.academic_year_id,
        class_id: r.class_id,
        subject_id: r.subject_id,
        teacher_id: r.teacher_id,
        default_lab_id: r.default_lab_id,
        created_at: r.created_at ? new Date(r.created_at).toISOString() : undefined,
        class_name: r.class_name || r.class_id,
        subject_code: r.subject_code || '',
        subject_name: r.subject_name || r.subject_id,
        teacher_name: r.teacher_name || undefined,
        lab_name: r.lab_name || undefined,
      })),
    }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    console.error('Failed to get class_subjects from database:', msg)
    return { success: false, data: [], error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Assigns a subject to a class in public.class_subjects junction table.
 */
export async function assignSubjectToClassAction(
  data: AssignSubjectToClassInput
): Promise<{ success: boolean; data?: ClassSubjectRecord; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
      return { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized: Only administrative faculty can assign subjects to classes.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()

    // Determine current academic year if not provided
    let yearId = data.academic_year_id
    if (!yearId) {
      const yearRes = await pool.query(
        `SELECT id FROM public.academic_years WHERE is_current = true LIMIT 1;`
      )
      yearId = yearRes.rows[0]?.id || null
    }

    // Check if mapping already exists
    const checkRes = await pool.query(
      `
      SELECT id FROM public.class_subjects 
      WHERE class_id = $1 AND subject_id = $2 
        AND (academic_year_id = $3 OR ($3 IS NULL AND academic_year_id IS NULL))
      LIMIT 1;
      `,
      [data.class_id, data.subject_id, yearId]
    )

    let recordId: string
    if (checkRes.rows.length > 0) {
      // Update existing mapping
      recordId = checkRes.rows[0].id
      await pool.query(
        `
        UPDATE public.class_subjects
        SET teacher_id = $1, default_lab_id = $2
        WHERE id = $3;
        `,
        [data.teacher_id || null, data.default_lab_id || null, recordId]
      )
    } else {
      // Insert new mapping
      const insertRes = await pool.query(
        `
        INSERT INTO public.class_subjects (academic_year_id, class_id, subject_id, teacher_id, default_lab_id, created_at)
        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, now())
        RETURNING id;
        `,
        [yearId, data.class_id, data.subject_id, data.teacher_id || null, data.default_lab_id || null]
      )
      recordId = insertRes.rows[0].id
    }

    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/schedules')

    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    console.error('Failed to assign subject to class:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

/**
 * Removes a class_subjects junction mapping with foreign key protection & RBAC guard.
 */
export async function removeClassSubjectAction(
  classSubjectId: string
): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const scope = await getServerUserScope()
    if (!scope.isPrivileged || scope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
      return { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized: Only administrative faculty can remove class subject assignments.' }
    }

    const pool = getPgPool()

    // 1. Foreign Key Reference Check: Guard against orphan or corrupted practical logs
    const logCheck = await pool.query(
      `SELECT count(*)::int AS log_count FROM public.practical_logs WHERE class_subject_id = $1;`,
      [classSubjectId]
    )
    const logCount = logCheck.rows[0]?.log_count || 0
    if (logCount > 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `Cannot delete class-subject assignment: ${logCount} historical practical log(s) reference this assignment. Unassign the teacher instead to preserve official audit records.`,
      }
    }

    // 2. Transactional safe delete: cleans associated schedules and deletes mapping cleanly
    const client = await pool.connect()
    try {
      await client.query('BEGIN')
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.schedules WHERE class_subject_id = $1;`, [classSubjectId])
      await client.query(`DELETE FROM public.class_subjects WHERE id = $1;`, [classSubjectId])
      await client.query('COMMIT')
    } catch (e) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      throw e
    } finally {
      client.release()
    }

    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/schedules')

    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error'
    console.error('Failed to remove class_subject assignment:', msg)
    return { success: false, error: msg }
  }
}

CODE FILE: app\actions\logs.ts
